Code is available at https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal.

</details>


### [74] [Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning for Unsupervised Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15220)
*Moyao Tian,Shijia Liu,Yan Yang,Xin Yuan,Minshi Chen,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构-语义互学习（SSRL）框架，通过细粒度结构解耦（FSD）提取身体部位特征作为空间锚点，并设计闭环语义校准（CSC）机制过滤伪标签噪声，将开环聚类关联转化为自校正闭环系统，解决无监督可见光-红外行人重识别中的模态差异和噪声传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跨模态行人重识别方法依赖模糊的全局特征，且在开环方式下伪标签噪声会不受控地传播，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 1) 细粒度结构解耦（FSD）：提取判别性身体部位基元作为可靠的空间锚点，补充全局轮廓信息；2) 闭环语义校准（CSC）：每个epoch重建共享语义原型并反馈到训练循环中，过滤伪标签噪声后再进行下一轮聚类。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB上超越现有无监督方法，且在RegDB上超越多个有监督方法。

Conclusion: 结构学习与语义学习之间的互反馈机制能够实现鲁棒的跨模态表示，将开环关联转化为自校正闭环系统是有效的。

Abstract: Unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification (USVI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality gap and the lack of cross-modal identity annotations. Progressive association paradigms have been proposed to gradually bridge the gap, but they suffer from two critical bottlenecks: reliance on ambiguous global representations and unchecked propagation of pseudo-label noise in an open-loop manner. To address these issues, we propose Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a framework that transforms open-loop association into a self-correcting closed-loop system. Structurally, we introduce Fine-grained Structural Decoupling (FSD) to extract discriminative body-part primitives as reliable spatial anchors, complementing ambiguous holistic silhouettes with spatially consistent structural details. Semantically, we design a Closed-loop Semantic Calibration (CSC) mechanism that reconstructs shared semantic prototypes at each epoch and feeds them back into the training loop, effectively filtering pseudo-label noise before the next clustering cycle. Through the reciprocal interaction between structural and semantic learning, SSRL achieves robust cross-modal representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the competitive performance of SSRL against state-of-the-art USVI-ReID methods on both SYSU-MM01 and RegDB, notably surpassing several supervised counterparts on RegDB.

</details>


### [75] [Divergent Gaze Patterns in Artistic Viewing: Spatial and Temporal Signatures of Attention Across Autistic Individuals, Artists, and Neurotypical Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15227)
*Mohammed Amine Kerkouri,Daphné Senggaran,Renaud Jusiak,Océane Lehmann,Marouane Tliba,Claire Wardak,Emmanuelle Houy-Durand,Shasha Morel-Kohlmeyer,Aladine Chetouani,Nadia Aguillon-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过眼动追踪实验比较了自闭症成人、训练有素的艺术家和神经典型者在欣赏绘画时的视觉探索模式，发现艺术家与神经典型者在空间和时间维度上几乎无法区分，而自闭症组表现出独特的注意力特征：空间探索广度与艺术家相似，但时间模式不同（注视更短、停留更少、扫描路径最不自治），形成了一种群体特有的注意力剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自闭症眼动研究多聚焦社交场景而非艺术，且只分析眼睛落在哪里而忽略了何时及按什么顺序看。理解不同人群如何视觉探索艺术对认知科学和无障碍设计都具有重要意义。

Method: 三组被试（ASD、艺术家、神经典型者）各观看30幅绘画15秒。引入两个互补的分析轴：空间轴（用六种显著性指标比较注视密度图）和时间轴（用MultiMatch、ScanMatch、FDISS、DTW比较扫描路径）。采用色散阈值算法统一提取注视点。

Result: (i)艺术家与神经典型者在空间（密度图相关CC=0.96）和时间上几乎不可区分，而ASD与两者均不同。(ii)ASD注意力分离：空间探索广度与艺术家相似，但时间模式不同（注视更短、停留更少、扫描路径最不自治）。(iii)ASD注视在任何指标上都不特别像艺术家，反而略微更接近神经典型者。

Conclusion: 自闭症患者对艺术的观看是一种在空间和时间上均独特的群体特异性注意力剖面，这启示需要建立基于人群条件的美学注意力模型。

Abstract: How different populations visually explore artworks bears on cognitive science and on accessibility design, yet most eye-tracking work in autism has used social scenes rather than art, and has analysed where the eyes land while ignoring when and in what order. We present a comparative free-viewing study across three groups, autistic adults (ASD), trained artists, and neurotypical observers, who each viewed 30 paintings for 15s. We introduce a directed, metric-grounded framework that compares groups along two complementary axes: a spatial axis, in which one group's fixation-density map predicts another's fixations under six saliency metrics (AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, Information Gain); and a temporal axis, in which individual scanpaths are compared with MultiMatch, ScanMatch, a foveal-disc IoU score (FDISS), and dynamic time warping (DTW). Fixations are extracted uniformly for all groups with a dispersion-threshold algorithm. Three results converge. (i)Artists and neurotypicals are almost indistinguishable in both space (density-map correlation CC=0.96) and time (they form the most alignable scanpath pair), whereas ASD gaze diverges from both. (ii)ASD attention is dissociated: it matches artists' wide spatial exploration (dispersion, explored area) but carries a distinct temporal signature, shorter fixations, less dwell, and the most idiosyncratic (least self-consistent) scanpaths of any group. (iii)ASD gaze is not selectively artist-like on any metric; if anything it is marginally closer to neurotypical. Together these findings indicate that autistic viewing of art is a distinct, group-specific attentional profile in both space and time, and they motivate population-conditioned models of aesthetic attention. We release all analysis code and per-stimulus results.

</details>


### [76] [CRISP: Constrained Refinement via Iterative Squeezing Process for Robust Medical Image Segmentation under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15231)
*Yizhou Fang,Pujin Cheng,Yixiang Liu,Xiaoying Tang,Longxi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRISP框架，利用概率排名的稳定性在分布偏移下生成鲁棒的空间先验，无需目标域数据或测试时参数更新，实现源域仅分割模型在未知环境下的鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的分布偏移严重阻碍AI模型临床转化，现有域适应方法（模拟偏移或伪监督）难以应对开放世界中无限且不可预见的真实分布偏移。

Method: 基于"正区域排名稳定性"假设，通过潜在特征扰动提取扰动不变的高精度(HP)核心和至少一次扰动下保留前景的高召回(HR)支撑区域，递归细化后迭代训练逐步逼近最终分割。

Result: 在多中心心脏MRI和CT肺血管分割任务上，HD95分别降低0.14(7.0%)、1.90(13.1%)和8.39(38.9%)像素，显著优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: CRISPR为目标域不可见的源域仅分割提供了一种有效的鲁棒性提升方案，验证了排名稳定性作为分布偏移下鲁棒先验的有效性。

Abstract: Distribution shift in medical imaging remains a central bottleneck for the clinical translation of medical AI. Failure to address it can lead to severe performance degradation in unseen environments and exacerbate health inequities. Existing methods for domain adaptation are inherently limited by exhausting predefined possibilities through simulated shifts or pseudo-supervision. Such strategies struggle in the open-ended and unpredictable real world, where distribution shifts are effectively infinite. To address this challenge, we adopt the "Rank Stability of Positive Regions" as a working assumption under distribution shift, and use it to derive robust spatial hints for source-only segmentation. Guided by this assumption, we propose CRISP, a model-agnostic framework that, unlike deployment-time adaptation, requires no test-time parameter updates and no target-domain data--a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that segments with frozen weights. Rather than using ranking to directly output masks, CRISP exploits the stability of probability rankings under distribution shift to derive robust spatial priors. Via latent feature perturbation, perturbation-invariant high-grade regions define a high-precision (HP) core, while voxels that remain potentially foreground under at least one perturbation define a high-recall (HR) support; these dual priors are then recursively refined under perturbation. We then design an iterative training framework that progressively squeezes HP and HR toward the final segmentation. Extensive evaluations on multi-center cardiac MRI and CT-based lung vessel segmentation demonstrate CRISP's superior robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods with striking HD95 reductions of up to 0.14 (7.0% improvement), 1.90 (13.1% improvement), and 8.39 (38.9% improvement) pixels across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts, respectively.

</details>


### [77] [ARMOR++: Agentic Orchestration of a Multi-Domain Primitive Set for Transferable Attacks on Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15246)
*Christos Korgialas,Gabriel Lee Jun Rong,Dion Jia Xu Ho,Pai Chet Ng,Xiaoxiao Miao,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ARMOR++多智能体框架，利用Qwen2.5-VL视觉语言模型提供空间语义先验、Qwen3大语言模型协调五种攻击原语，通过熵正则化扰动混合实现高转移性的深度伪造检测绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在黑盒对抗转移攻击下可靠性严重下降，而现有转移攻击缺乏语义感知能力，在无查询约束下从CNN代理模型迁移到Transformer目标模型时效果不佳。

Method: ARMOR++框架：1) Qwen2.5-VL提供空间语义先验；2) Qwen3 LLM协调原语选择、自适应超参数重参数化和熵正则化扰动混合；3) 集成五种互补原语（密集优化、显著性方法、空间变换、频域扰动、块结构修改）。

Result: 在AADD-2025基准上，ARMOR++在低质量和高质量图像场景下均显著优于现有基线方法，盲目标攻击成功率（ASR）大幅超越最先进智能体基线。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前深度伪造检测部署中存在显著的剩余可靠性差距，证明了智能体编排在识别潜在脆弱性方面的有效性。

Abstract: The reliability of deepfake detectors frequently degrades under black-box adversarial transfer, as these models often rely on fragile, architecture-dependent forensic cues. Existing transfer attacks often lack semantic awareness and struggle to maintain effectiveness under strict no-query constraints, particularly when perturbations are transferred from convolutional surrogates to transformer-based targets. To address these limitations, this paper introduces ARMOR++, a robust multi-agent framework designed for high-transferability deepfake evasion. The framework leverages the Qwen2.5-VL Vision-Language Model (VLM) to supply spatial semantic priors, while the Qwen3 Large Language Model (LLM) orchestrates primitive selection, adaptive hyperparameter reparameterization, and entropy-regularized perturbation mixing. By integrating five complementary primitives, spanning dense optimization, saliency-based methods, spatial transformations, frequency-domain perturbations, and block-structured modifications, ARMOR++ effectively targets heterogeneous inductive biases. Rigorous evaluation on the AADD-2025 benchmark demonstrates that ARMOR++ significantly outperforms existing agentic and non-agentic baselines across both low- and high-quality image regimes. Statistical analysis confirms a substantial gain in blind-target Attack Success Rate (ASR) over the state-of-the-art agentic baseline, with further performance advantages evidenced against non-agentic benchmarks and under robust defensive configurations. These findings highlight a significant residual reliability gap in current deepfake detector deployments and demonstrate the efficacy of agentic orchestration in identifying latent vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [78] [HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15255)
*Pengcheng Zhou,Xuanyu Liu,Yanchen Yin,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究VLM在地理定位中的地标偏差问题，提出偏差度量指标BI/BH和基准LandmarkBias-3K，并设计基于证据驱动推理的HoloGeo框架（含BF-30k数据集和多维奖励机制），在保持原有性能的同时显著提升鲁棒地理推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在地理定位中受地标偏差影响，要么忽略地理线索，要么形成虚假关联，导致定位不准确。缺乏对这一问题的系统量化和有效缓解方法。

Method: 1) 设计Bias Intensity (BI)和Bias Harmfulness (BH)两个量化指标；2) 构建LandmarkBias-3K基准测试；3) 提出HoloGeo框架，包含BF-30k高质量数据集（带结构化多证据无偏推理链）和多维奖励机制，鼓励对多样化视觉线索的均衡关注，实现证据驱动的联合推理。

Result: HoloGeo在IM2GPS3K和YFCC4k上保持优异性能，在LandmarkBias-3K上显著优于现有开源VLM，验证了鲁棒地理空间推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化多证据推理链和多维奖励，可以有效缓解VLM在地理定位中的地标偏差，提升定位可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.

</details>


### [79] [SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15265)
*Mingfei Chen,Zijun Cui,Ruoke Zhang,Hyeonggon Ryu,Eli Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBind 提出了一种全模态场景表示方法，通过结合全局语义嵌入和以物体为中心的语义-空间槽（slots），显式建模场景中的语义信息、空间属性及其不确定性，支持跨模态场景检索和物体定位，在真实双耳视听数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态编码器擅长实例级语义（有什么），但缺乏显式的空间结构（在哪里）。SceneBind旨在填补这一空白，实现兼具语义和3D空间理解的场景表示。

Method: 将每个场景表示为语义-空间实体：全局语义嵌入 + 物体级语义-空间槽（object-centric semantic-spatial slots）。提出SceneBind Matching方案，整合全局场景相似度与物体对齐，支持跨模态检索和物体定位。配套构建真实双耳视听数据集并设计对齐训练协议。

Result: 在场景检索和空间检索任务上达到SOTA，并在音频-视觉定位等下游任务上实现强零样本迁移。

Conclusion: SceneBind通过轻量级空间建模（仅额外少量token）即可兼容大规模预训练语义编码器，有效桥接了语义与空间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

</details>


### [80] [Motion-Conditioned Multi-View Fusion for Myocardial Infarction Localization from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15268)
*Guang Yang,Wentian Xu,Siyu Wang,Betty Raman,Lei Li,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MCF-Net，一种基于运动引导的多视图融合框架，通过融合心肌运动线索与基础模型表示，在极稀疏监督下实现心肌梗死节段级定位，达到72.4% F1和84.9%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心肌运动分析方法依赖手工特征或密集监督标注，标注成本高；基础模型在单视图上效果有限，节段级定位受视图依赖模糊性影响，尤其是在心尖视图上不可靠。

Method: 利用预训练Echo基础模型（EchoPrime）提取双视图视觉特征；仅用单张标注模板帧跨视频传递进行点跟踪实现极稀疏运动建模；运动导出的节段感知软掩码提供空间先验增强困难节段特征；运动条件融合机制跨视图整合运动与视觉信息。

Result: 节段级MI定位F1达72.4%，准确率84.9%，优于当前最优的运动-only、视觉-only及融合基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的运动引导多视图融合框架有效解决了视图模糊性问题，在极稀疏监督下大幅提升了心肌梗死节段定位性能。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) remains a leading cause of mortality worldwide. Echocardiography (Echo) is a widely available modality for MI assessment, where regional wall motion abnormality is a key indicator. Prior learning based methods for myocardial motion analysis often use handcrafted descriptors or densely supervised estimation, but the need for extensive annotation limits applicability. Foundation models have recently improved vision-based Echo analysis; however, most methods operate on single views and segment-level localization remains unreliable under view-dependent ambiguity, especially in apical views. To address this, we propose MCF-Net, a novel motion-guided multi-view fusion framework that fuses myocardial motion cues with foundation model representations to localize infarction. Visual features are extracted using EchoPrime, a pretrained Echo foundation model shared across dual views. Cardiac motion is modeled with extremely sparse supervision: a single annotated template frame is transferred across videos to initialize point tracking, avoiding dense labels. Motion-derived segment-aware soft masks provide coarse spatial priors that selectively enhance features for challenging myocardial segments. A motion-conditioned fusion mechanism then integrates motion and vision across views, refining predictions without overriding strong appearance cues. On segment-level MI localization, MCF-Net achieves 72.4\% F1 and 84.9\% accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art motion-only, vision-only, and fusion baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Online Neural Space Time Memory for Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15271)
*Baback Elmieh,Lynn Tsai,Zeman Li,Srinivas Kaza,Tiancheng Sun,Gabor Csapo,Ali Behrouz,Yuan Deng,Stephen Lombardi,Steven M. Seitz,Xuan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将记忆更新和记忆应用频率解耦的在线神经视角合成方法，通过周期性记忆更新+逐帧记忆应用+跨视角注意力处理动态形变，并引入Memory Loss和Memory Caching机制防止灾难性漂移，实现实时动态场景渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角流媒体视频的在线新视角合成面临维持长期记忆以重建临时遮挡区域与实时性约束之间的矛盾。现有TTT方法每帧都需要梯度记忆更新，计算成本高且长序列不稳定，而视频内容存在大量冗余。

Method: 1) 将记忆更新（周期性）与记忆应用（逐帧）解耦；2) 跨视角注意力对齐先前记忆状态与当前帧形变；3) 辅助Memory Loss强制场景内化；4) Memory Caching策略正则化活跃权重防止灾难性漂移。

Result: 在动态人体运动场景上实现实时、最先进的性能，并支持分钟量级的在线记忆。

Conclusion: 频率解耦策略有效平衡了长期记忆维持与实时计算约束，为在线动态场景新视角合成提供实用方案。

Abstract: Online novel view synthesis from multi-view streaming videos faces a fundamental trade-off: maintaining a persistent, long-horizon memory to reconstruct temporarily occluded regions while operating under strict real-time constraints. While Test-Time Training (TTT) offers a powerful memory mechanism, standard models mandate gradient-based memory updates at every frame to adapt to the changing motion in dynamic scenes. The computational cost of heavy memory updates precludes real-time application and can lead to instability over long contexts. Given that memory updates are more demanding than memory application and video content is largely redundant, we propose to decouple the frequencies of these two processes. Our approach performs periodic memory updates while applying the memory on a per-frame basis, using cross-view attention to manage deformations between the prior memory state and the current frame. To lock in the historical context, we introduce two critical mechanisms: an auxiliary Memory Loss that forces persistent internalization of the scene, and a Memory Caching strategy that regularizes active weights against catastrophic drift. Our method demonstrates real-time, state-of-the-art performance on scenes with dynamic human motion as well as minute-scale online memorization.

</details>


### [82] [MeanFlowNFT: Bringing Forward-Process RL to Average-Velocity Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15273)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jun Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将DiffusionNFT的强化学习框架扩展到MeanFlow生成器上，通过构建瞬时速度预测器实现平均速度采样的奖励优化，在图像和视频生成上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiffusionNFT等RL对齐方法适用于扩散/流模型，但MeanFlow生成器使用平均速度采样，与DiffusionNFT优化的瞬时速度不兼容，因此需要弥合这一差距，让MeanFlow也能享受RL带来的偏好对齐收益。

Method: 利用MeanFlow恒等式连接平均速度和瞬时速度，构建诱导的瞬时速度预测器，在其上施加DiffusionNFT目标函数进行奖励优化，但采样时仍保留平均速度以保持MeanFlow的少步生成优势。

Result: 在SD3.5-M上6/8指标超越先前SOTA；在Wan 2.1上4步达到VBench 84.33，超越50步LongCat-Video RL的82.57。

Conclusion: MeanFlowNFT成功将RL对齐引入MeanFlow生成器，继承了严格策略改进保证，并在少步生成场景下显著超越基线和大步数RL调优扩散模型。

Abstract: MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).

</details>


### [83] [Hierarchical Denoising For Multi-Step Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15278)
*Zezhong Qian,Xiaowei Chi,Chak-Wing Mak,Tianze Zhou,Ruibin Yuan,Yuhan Rui,Hengzhe Sun,Zhuoqun Wu,Yuming Li,Siyuan Qian,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HDR是一种层级去噪框架，将视频潜在表示组织成树状层级结构，实现从粗到细的多步视觉推理，在保持流式低延迟的同时显著提升推理成功率（34.22→60.29，相对提升76.2%），推理速度比双向扩散快54.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式自回归扩散模型缺乏多步推理能力，而双向扩散虽能全局修正但推理成本高，两者都难以在复杂推理任务中同时实现逻辑一致性和低延迟流式生成。

Method: 提出HDR框架，将视频潜在表示组织成树状层级结构：粗去噪层保留不确定假设用于全局规划，细去噪层逐步精化为具体视觉状态；设计稀疏层级注意力模式（SHAP）降低时序注意力成本。

Result: 在6个多步推理任务上，HDR成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对+76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56；流式延迟0.70秒/潜在，推理速度比双向扩散快54.2倍；仅用2%训练数据保留82.9%性能（双向扩散仅52.0%）；有真实机器人实验验证。

Conclusion: HDR通过层级去噪统一了因果视频生成中的多步推理与流式低延迟，在复杂推理任务上显著优于现有方法，并展示了在物理交互与世界建模中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Open-AoE: An Open Egocentric Manipulation Dataset and Toolchain for Embodied Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14183)
*Zishuo Li,Bowen Yang,Changtao Miao,Kai Zhu,Hao Chen,Qingze Guan,Zhengxing Wu,Wanke Zhan,Yang Sun,Zhiyi Huang,Zitong Shan,Zhenchao Jin,Jiadong Hong,Taowen Wang,Yushi Feng,You Liu,Yibo Wang,Yifan Yang,Zhaowen Zhou,Man Luo,Hao Cheng,Bo Zhang,Jianshu Li,Jiansheng Cai,Guocai Yao,Jize Zhang,Chenhao Lin,Renjing Xu,Lequan Yu,Chao Shen,Chunhua Shen,Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Open-AoE 是一个开放、社区驱动的自我中心操作数据集与工具链，包含约2000小时智能手机采集的操作视频，并提供文本标注、MANO手部姿态、相机轨迹和原子动作标注，以及从原始录制到模型训练的全流程处理工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心操作视频资源难以同时满足低成本大规模采集、操作级别结构化标注和可复用的机器人学习工具，缺少从数据采集到模型训练的完整开源基础设施。

Method: 通过智能手机低成本连续采集自然环境中的人体操作视频（500+贡献者，400+手机），构建数据处理流水线（时间动作分割、语义标注、手部重建、相机轨迹重建），并配套下游工具链（可视化、跨本体重定位、模型数据转换、VLA/WAM/World Model训练脚本）。

Result: 发布了约2000小时自我中心操作视频数据集，配有文本标注、MANO手部姿态、相机轨迹和原子动作标签，并提供完整的处理与训练工具链。

Conclusion: Open-AoE通过可扩展采集、结构化处理和下游适配的整合，降低了数据贡献与复用的门槛，为具身模型训练、人-机器人迁移和世界建模提供了实用的开源基础设施。

Abstract: Egocentric videos of human manipulation provide scalable supervision for embodied intelligence, yet existing resources rarely combine low-cost continuous capture, manipulation-level structured annotations, and reusable tools for robot learning. We present Open-AoE, an open, community-oriented egocentric manipulation dataset and toolchain spanning the full pipeline from smartphone capture to model training. Its first release contains approximately 2,000 hours of manipulation video collected in natural environments by 500+ contributors using 400+ smartphones. The dataset provides text annotations, MANO-based hand poses, camera trajectories, and temporally localized atomic actions. Open-AoE further includes a data processing pipeline that transforms raw recordings into structured samples through temporal action segmentation, semantic annotation, hand reconstruction, and camera trajectory reconstruction. Meanwhile, we provide a separate downstream toolchain supports visualization, cross-embodiment retargeting, model-specific data conversion, and training recipes for VLA policies, WAMs, and World Models. By integrating scalable capture, structured processing, and downstream adaptation, Open-AoE reduces the barriers to both data contribution and reuse, providing practical open infrastructure for embodied model training, human-to-robot transfer, and world modeling.

</details>


### [85] [Never Too Late for Force: Accelerating VLA Post-Training with Reactive Force Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14236)
*Yi Wang,Wendi Chen,Zimo Wen,Han Xue,Xueqi Li,Wenye Yu,Zhijie Chen,Hao Yang,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIFT是一个力感知的后训练框架，通过在预训练VLA策略中嫁接反应式动作专家并结合因果力记忆与零初始化交叉注意力，实现接触状态下的力反馈驱动动作更新，同时用DAgger在线循环处理策略依赖的分布偏移，在毛巾折叠、书本插入、汉诺塔放置等任务中超越纯视觉后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA策略在进入接触状态（场景遮挡、深度模糊、小力误差导致偏离离线演示分布）时表现不佳，需要为策略注入接触反应能力而不破坏其通用操作知识。

Method: 在预训练VLA的动作头旁嫁接一个反应式动作专家（从预训练权重初始化），通过因果力记忆编码最近6D末端力信息，使用零初始化交叉注意力将力信息注入动作生成，实现执行中动作刷新；同时耦合在线DAgger循环，在离线任务对齐数据和人工校正的在线轨迹混合上训练。

Result: 在毛巾折叠、书本插入、汉诺塔放置三个接触密集任务上，LIFT相比纯视觉后训练学习更快、最终性能更高；消融实验表明反应式力记忆与在线校正数据对鲁棒接触操作均至关重要。

Conclusion: 力感知后训练有效弥补VLA在接触状态下的短板，因果力记忆与在线DAgger是VLA接触反应能力的关键组件。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong language-conditioned manipulation knowledge, but they remain largely vision-driven and can struggle once manipulation enters contact states where the scene is occluded, depth is ambiguous, or small force errors push execution off the offline demonstration distribution. We present LIFT (Late Reactive Injection of Force for VLA Post-Training), a force-aware post-training framework that adds contact reactivity to a pretrained VLA policy while preserving its general manipulation knowledge. LIFT grafts a reactive action expert beside the original action expert, initializes it from pretrained action weights, and injects recent 6D end-effector force through causal force memory and zero-initialized cross attention, enabling actions to be refreshed during execution. To address the policy-dependent distribution shift of contact feedback, LIFT further couples reactive force injection with an online DAgger loop that trains on a mixture of offline task-alignment data and human-corrected online rollouts. Across towel folding, book insertion, and Hanoi ring placement, LIFT learns faster and reaches higher performance than vision-only post-training, while ablations show that reactive force memory and online corrective data are both important for robust contact-rich manipulation. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [86] [An offline approach to fNIRS-guided reinforcement learning for robot behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14393)
*Julia Santaniello,Madelaine Brower,Benson Jiang,Donatello Sassaroli,Robert Jacob,Jivko Sinapov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 探索使用fNIRS脑信号作为人类反馈信号来增强强化学习训练机器人行为。在仿真中比较了被动观察与主动演示两种交互模式，通过参数增强方式将神经信号融入RL算法，验证了该方法在提升轨迹优先级和状态-动作Q值学习方面的有效性，并支持离线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Human-in-the-loop RL依赖显式用户反馈（如按键、评分），但这种方式对用户负担大且效率低。本文希望探索能否利用大脑信号（fNIRS）作为更自然、更隐式的反馈信号，来调节机器人学习过程，降低用户交互成本。

Method: 使用fNIRS采集脑信号，采用参数增强（而非替换）方式将神经信号融入RL算法：分别增强轨迹优先级和状态-动作Q值。在仿真环境中比较被动观察和主动演示两种交互任务。还研究了模型粒度和噪声对学习的影响，以及离线学习的可行性。

Result: 神经信号能够有效改善学习效果：增强轨迹优先级和Q值时均观察到性能提升。该框架还能成功从离线数据中学习，为实时BCI设置不可行或数据有限的情况提供了实用替代方案。

Conclusion: fNIRS脑信号可以作为有效的隐式反馈信号用于机器人RL训练，参数增强方式比直接替换更优，且离线学习能力增加了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop Reinforcement Learning has become a popular approach to training, finetuning, and aligning robot behavior with user preferences. Our paper explores the feasibility of using brain signals via functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) to modulate robot learning in simulation. We compare agents trained on passive (observational) versus active (demonstrative) interaction tasks, and test multiple methods for enhancing the RL algorithm with the neural signal, focusing on parameter augmentation rather than replacement. We further examine how model granularity and noise affect agent learning. Our results show that this framework is effective: the neural signal improves learning when augmenting trajectory priorities and state-action q-values. Additionally, the framework learns successfully from offline data, offering a practical alternative for settings where real-time BCI setups are impractical or only limited data is available.

</details>


### [87] [ConFlow: Constraints-Guided Learning with Flow Matching for Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14424)
*Nutan Chen,Jianxiang Feng,Marvin Alles,Botond Cseke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConFlow 是一种将约束信息（通过可微势垒/代价函数）直接融入 Flow Matching 训练目标的框架，并用条件高斯过程替代标准高斯源分布，利用不可行演示作为负监督，在双机器人导航任务中实现了更低的碰撞率和更高的轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动生成中常有额外约束（如避障、平滑性），但标准 Flow Matching 仅在数据上训练，约束只能在推理时通过 guidance 施加，导致训练-推理不一致。

Method: 1) 将可微约束函数（barrier/cost）加入训练损失；2) 用条件高斯过程替代标准高斯源分布以满足边界条件和平滑性；3) 利用不可行演示作为负监督样本。

Result: 在双机器人导航任务中，ConFlow 相比标准 Flow Matching 基线（无论是否带推理时 guidance）实现了更低的碰撞率和更高的轨迹质量。

Conclusion: 训练时约束集成是弥合生成运动模型中训练-推理鸿沟的有效方法。

Abstract: In recent years Flow Matching has become a prominent method for generative modeling robot motion generation. In its generic form Flow Matching is an ODE-based neural sampler that is trained by regressing empirical flow fields associated with motion samples as data. However, in robot motion generation we often have additional constraints that might not be present in the collected data. The majority of current approaches train the flow on the available data and use inference-time guidance to enforce task-specific constraints. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{ConFlow}, a constraint-guided flow matching framework that incorporates constraint information directly into the training objective via differentiable barrier or cost functions. To address design specifications such as smoothness and boundary conditions, we propose replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution used in flow matching training with a conditional Gaussian Process. Our approach also uses infeasible demonstrations as negative supervision, improving constraint satisfaction without requiring additional expert data. Experiments on a two-robot navigation task demonstrate that ConFlow achieves lower collision rates and higher trajectory quality than standard flow matching baselines, with or without inference-time guidance. These results validate training-time constraint integration as an effective approach to closing the training--inference gap in generative motion models.

</details>


### [88] [Motion Planning with Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14455)
*Zhilin He,Bowei Li,Jianlin Dou,Yuner Zhang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MD-COAS方法，通过融合iALM软扩散先验和基于凸可行集(CFS)的硬投影算子，并引入自适应调度机制，统一了安全约束的软硬两种范式，解决了模型基扩散方法在高度非凸约束环境中安全执行与扩散调度耦合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型基扩散(MBD)方法在约束环境中要么使用软可行性扩散先验，要么使用硬投影算子，缺乏统一框架；且固定安全执行方式，忽略了扩散调度变化的影响，导致安全性与效率受限。

Method: 将不精确增广拉格朗日方法(iALM)作为软扩散先验与凸可行集(CFS)硬投影算子统一到一个框架中，并自适应调度和协同优化安全执行与扩散调度。

Result: 在高度非凸2D基准和7自由度机械臂避障任务上，相比基线方法实现了更高的安全率与成功率、更快的收敛速度和更低的最终代价。

Conclusion: 统一软硬约束范式并引入自适应调度能有效提升高度非凸约束下单机器人运动规划的 safety、成功率与效率。

Abstract: Single-Robot Motion Planning (SRMP) in highly non-convex constrained environments, where robots must satisfy collision-free guarantees, dynamic feasibility, and task-related constraints, is challenging under complex constraints and computational limits. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast the SRMP as trajectory optimization that samples from a posterior over trajectories, using known dynamics, and analytically estimates the score function from rollout samples to guide diffusion denoising toward a low-cost, clean trajectory without demonstration learning. While existing works further adapt MBD to constrained environments and showcase promising performance, they are still limited by (1) enforcing safety either via soft feasibility diffusion priors or hard projection operators, but lack a unified framework to integrate both, and (2) fixing safety enforcement to neglect the changing of diffusion scheduling. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling (MD-COAS) for SRMP that unifies the inexact Augmented Lagrangian Method (iALM) soft diffusion prior with a Convex Feasible Set (CFS)-based hard projection operator, and adaptively schedules and co-optimizes safety enforcement, along with diffusion scheduling. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves higher safety \& success rates, faster convergence, and lower final costs than baseline planners on randomly generated highly non-convex 2D benchmarks and a 7-DoF robot arm avoidance task.

</details>


### [89] [Mixed-Agent Museum Tour Guide Design Improves Gendered Learning Outcomes and Visitor Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14468)
*Annette M. Masterson,Wonse Jo,Helena C. Sieh,Lionel P. Robert,,Dawn Tilbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合智能导览系统，将实体机器人与投影虚拟代理结合，通过双代理对话式交互提升博物馆参观体验。30人被试内实验表明，不同会话风格和配置下参与度和体验质量一致，但混合代理条件显著提升了女性学习表现，且所有性别参与者均偏好混合代理团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博物馆导览机器人多为单一实体代理，互动丰富度有限。作者希望通过融合实体机器人与虚拟投影代理，在单一硬件平台上实现类似两个移动代理的交互丰富度，以提升参观者的参与度、体验质量和学习效果。

Method: 开发混合智能导览系统（实体机器人+投影虚拟代理），采用双代理对话式交互风格。通过30人被试内实验，对比不同会话风格和代理配置，使用问卷、行为传感器和访谈收集数据，分析参与度、体验质量和学习表现。

Result: 各条件下参与度和体验质量一致；学习表现存在显著的性别调节差异——混合代理条件提升了女性参与者的学习表现，但对男性无显著影响；访谈中所有性别参与者均更偏好混合代理团队，认为互动是关键因素。

Conclusion: 混合代理导览系统在学习效果上存在性别差异效应，但整体用户体验良好且受用户偏好。未来可进一步探索双代理对话风格对学习的影响机制及个性化适配。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly integrated into everyday contexts, including museums, where they can both entertain and educate visitors. To enhance visitor experience and engagement, we present a novel mixed-agent tour guide system that combines a physical robot with a projected virtual agent that actively participates in the tour through conversation and interaction, achieving the interaction richness of two mobile agents from a single platform. We validate the system through a within-subjects study with 30 participants to assess engagement, quality of experience, and learning performance. Participants experienced different conversational styles and agent configurations, and data were collected via surveys, behavioral sensors, and interviews. Results showed that engagement and quality of experience remained consistent across conditions. Learning performance revealed a significant gender-moderated difference: the mixed-agent conditions improved learning performance for female participants. This suggests that the proposed dyadic conversational style in this paper influenced learning performance differently by gender. Nonetheless, in interviews, participants reported a greater preference for mixed-agent teams regardless of gender, citing interaction as a key factor in their experience.

</details>


### [90] [MIDAS Hand: Modular low-Impedance Direct-drive Anthropomorphic Sensing Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14487)
*Alvin Zhu,Mingzhang Zhu,Beom Jun Kim,Quanyou Wang,Jose Victor S. H. Ramos,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIDAS Hand 是一款低成本、开源、类人尺寸的灵巧手，配备集成触觉传感，16个自由度（13个主动），283个三轴触觉传感单元，总重700g，物料成本低于3000美元，可3D打印组装，并提供了完整的软件栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手硬件在类人形态、易制造维护、触觉传感和成本之间难以兼顾，限制了灵巧操作研究的发展。

Method: 设计并制造了一款直驱、低反向驱动力矩的16自由度（13主动）灵巧手，集成283个三轴触觉传感单元，全部采用3D打印组件，并提供完整软硬件开源栈（设计文件、API、仿真模型、遥操作管线）。

Result: 通过工作空间分析、抓取分类、负载与可靠性测试、反向驱动力测量及带触觉感知的遥操作演示验证了其性能，证明是一个平衡、可复现的灵巧操作研究平台。

Conclusion: MIDAS Hand 为触觉灵巧操作和人到机器人数据收集提供了一个兼顾形态、成本、传感和可维护性的开源硬件平台。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited not only by algorithms but by a shortage of accessible hand hardware that combines human-scale morphology, ease of manufacturing or maintenance, tactile sensing, and practical cost. Existing dexterous hands tend to optimize some of these properties at the expense of others. We present MIDAS Hand, a low-cost, open-source, human-scale dexterous hand with integrated tactile sensing for manipulation research. MIDAS Hand provides 16 total degrees of freedom (DoF) with 13 active DoF, directly driven actuation with measurably low backdrive torque, and 283 three-axis tactile taxels in a compact 700 g package with a bill of materials under 3,000 USD. Built from 3D-printed components, it assembles in under three hours while providing the strength, repeatability, and maintainability needed for repeated real-world experiments. Alongside the hardware, we release a full stack: design files, build documentation, control and tactile Python APIs, simulation models, and retargeting and teleoperation pipelines. We characterize MIDAS Hand through workspace and grasp-taxonomy analysis, payload and reliability tests, backdrivability measurements, and teleoperation demonstrations with tactile sensing, showing that it offers a balanced, reproducible platform for tactile dexterous manipulation and human-to-robot data collection. Project page: https://midas-hand.com

</details>


### [91] [Safe Execution of RL Policies Via Acceleration-Based CBF-QP Constraint Enforcement for Real-World Robotic Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14488)
*Bastien Muraccioli,Alice Cariou,Pierre-Alexandre Leziart,Mathieu Celerier,Arnaud Demont,Gentiane Venture,Mehdi Benallegue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Acc-CBF-QP 安全滤波器，通过基于加速度的二次规划约束屏障函数，在不修改训练的情况下，将任意 RL 策略的输出实时投影到安全集内，在 7-DoF Kinova Gen3 机械臂和 19-DoF Unitree H1 人形机器人上显著减少约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略在部署时缺乏安全保证，尤其是在腿式机器人和机械臂接近安全边界运行时，分布外状态会导致失败。

Method: 加速度基二次规划安全滤波器 (Acc-CBF-QP)，利用控制屏障函数将 RL 策略的原始动作约束到安全集内，统一优化框架下处理关节位置、速度、力矩和碰撞约束，并引入 TorqueTask 和 Forward Dynamics Task 两种任务以调节安全-性能权衡。

Result: 在真实 H1 人形机器人上，Safe-RL 策略违反率从 10.04 violations/s 降至 0.80 violations/s（降低 92%）；在 Kinova Gen3 上完全消除违反；在无违反区域保留原始 RL 任务性能；在激进速度指令下延长生存时间。

Conclusion: Acc-CBF-QP 是一种即插即用的安全滤波器，能有效保障 RL 策略在真实机器人上的安全执行，且无需修改训练过程，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated remarkable capabilities for solving complex robotic control problems, but its lack of safety guarantees severely limits deployment on hardware. In particular, as legged robots and manipulators often operate near safety-critical boundaries, out-of-distribution states can lead to failure upon deployment. To address this, we introduce Acc-CBF-QP, an acceleration-based Quadratic Program (QP) safety filter using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) that constrains any RL policy onto a safe set at runtime without modifying training. The method applies to unconstrained and Safe-RL policies, and enforces joint position, velocity, torque, and collision constraints within a unified optimization framework. A key contribution is the formulation of RL+QP tasks that regulate deviation from the RL command when constraints would otherwise be violated. We introduce a TorqueTask, minimizing torque deviation, and a Forward Dynamics Task, minimizing induced acceleration deviation, thus providing principled control over safety-performance trade-offs. Experiments on a 7-DoF Kinova Gen3 manipulator and a 19-DoF Unitree H1 humanoid, both in simulation and on hardware, highlight substantial reductions in constraint violations. On the real H1 hardware, a Safe-RL policy alone yielded 10.04 violations/s, which were reduced by 92% to 0.80 violations/s when augmented with Acc-CBF-QP. On the Kinova Gen3, Acc-CBF-QP fully eliminated violations. Nominal task performance of the RL objective is preserved in violation-free regimes. Under aggressive velocity commands on H1, Acc-CBF-QP improves execution by preventing constraint-induced shutdowns, yielding longer survival times. The full pipeline is open-source.

</details>


### [92] [DRIFT: Drift and Aggregation for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14507)
*Yining Xing,Zhiyuan Liu,Zehong Ke,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种固定深度轨迹规划器，在紧凑的潜空间中一步生成48个多假设轨迹候选，并通过轻量级聚合头直接预测最终轨迹，无需轨迹级质量标签。在NAVSIM navtest上达到89.6 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，聚合模块仅需10.82ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端轨迹规划需要表示多种可行驾驶行为，但将候选集转换为最终轨迹仍然是一个关键设计问题。现有proposal-based方法在聚合阶段存在效率和质量瓶颈。

Method: DRIFT包含两个核心组件：1) DRIFT Decoder：在紧凑轨迹潜空间中一步生成48个proposal特征（32个alpha=0.5, 16个alpha=0.9）；2) Aggregation Head：将proposal特征与场景、导航、自车状态信息融合，直接预测最终轨迹。训练使用专家轨迹模仿和地图边界正则化器。

Result: NAVSIM navtest上PDMS 89.6, EPDMS 90.4。聚合模块10.82ms (RTX 4090)，全模型推理66.43ms。在可行驶区域合规性和自车进度方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 一步潜空间proposal生成加直接聚合为多假设运动规划提供了高效且有效的新范式。

Abstract: End-to-end trajectory planners need to represent multiple plausible driving behaviors while producing a single executable trajectory under real-time constraints. Proposal-based approaches address this ambiguity by generating multiple candidates, but converting the proposal set into a final plan remains a key design problem. We present DRIFT, a fixed-depth planner that combines one-step drifting in a compact trajectory latent space with scene-aware proposal aggregation. Conditioned on features from a pretrained visual encoder, the DRIFT Decoder generates 48 proposal features in a single batched pass, with 32 samples at alpha=0.5 and 16 samples at alpha=0.9. A lightweight Aggregation Head integrates these features with scene, navigation, and ego-state information and directly predicts the final trajectory without requiring trajectory-level quality labels for aggregation. Its output is trained with expert-trajectory imitation and a map-derived boundary regularizer that penalizes waypoints outside the drivable polygon and inside waypoints near its boundary. On NAVSIM navtest, DRIFT achieves 89.6 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, with strong drivable-area compliance and ego progress among the methods compared. The proposal-generation and aggregation module runs in 10.82 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while full-model inference including the visual backbone takes 66.43 ms. These results show that one-step latent proposal generation and direct aggregation provide an efficient design for multi-hypothesis motion planning.

</details>


### [93] [Communication-Efficient Relative Pose Estimation with Vision Foundation Models for Ephemeral Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14539)
*Qihang Li,Jo-Hao Huang,Jiewen Liu,Suyoung Kang,Hao Zhang,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CERPE是一个系统级框架，利用视觉基础模型协同估计自运动和机器人间相对位姿，通过固定大小描述符共享减少原始观测交换，并利用度量化自运动传播处理无视觉重叠场景，实现通信高效的短暂协作感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相对位姿估计方法依赖全局参考系、假设持续视觉重叠或通信成本过高，无法适用于短暂交互窗口和有限带宽的真实多机器人协作场景。

Method: - 使用连续共享的固定大小描述符来门控事件触发的原始图像请求，减少不必要的原始观测交换；- 无重叠时通过度量化自运动传播机器人间相对位姿；- 协调视觉基础模型联合估计自运动和相对位姿。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验表明，CERPE在短暂协作感知中相比基线方法提升了6-DoF相对位姿估计精度。

Conclusion: CERPE通过通信高效的设计，使得多机器人在短暂交互、有限带宽和视觉遮挡等实际约束下仍能保持相对位姿估计能力，适用于短暂的协作感知场景。

Abstract: Relative pose estimation is a fundamental capability for collaborative perception and coordination in multi-robot systems. However, robots encountering each other in real-world environments often operate in short interaction windows and must operate under limited communication bandwidth with intermittent or missing visual overlap caused by occlusions or limited fields of view. Existing approaches typically rely on global reference frames, assume sustained view overlap, or incur prohibitive communication costs, thereby limiting their applicability to ephemeral collaborative perception. To address these challenges, we introduce communication-efficient relative pose estimation (CERPE), a system-level framework that coordinates vision foundation models to jointly estimate ego-motion and inter-robot relative pose. CERPE reduces unnecessary raw-observation exchange by using continuously shared fixed-size descriptors to gate event-triggered raw-image requests independently of pose estimation. Non-overlapping encounters are handled by propagating inter-robot relative poses through metrically scaled ego-motion, thus maintaining relative pose estimates even in the absence of visual overlap. Experiments in simulation and real-world robots show that CERPE improves 6-DoF relative pose estimation over selected baselines in ephemeral collaborative perception.

</details>


### [94] [SafeRelBench: A Spatial-Relation-Aware Benchmark for Process-Level Safety in VLM-Driven Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14543)
*Huaigang Yang,Ya Li,Min Ren,Bo Dai,Zhenliang Zhang,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFERELBENCH是一个空间关系感知的安全基准，包含507个可执行评估样本（248个空间关系样本+259个非空间控制样本），用于评估VLM驱动的具身智能体在执行任务过程中的过程级安全合规性。实验发现七个开源和闭源VLM具身智能体在任务成功率和过程级安全之间存在显著差距——模型常完成任务但违反安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身安全评估主要关注静态风险识别、不安全指令拒绝或最终任务完成，但未能充分研究由空间关系（支撑、包含、邻近）引起的过程级安全故障——即动作如何随时间改变物理场景并引入风险。

Method: 构建SAFERELBENCH基准，包含507个可执行评估样本（248个空间关系样本+259个非空间控制样本），在风险动作前明确测试智能体是否满足安全条件，使空间关系成为具身安全评估的核心维度。

Result: 评估了七个开源和闭源VLM驱动的具身智能体，发现任务成功与过程级安全合规之间存在显著差距——模型常完成任务但违反过程级安全约束。

Conclusion: 安全的具身智能不仅需要更强的感知和规划能力，还需要关于物体关系如何塑造交互过程中风险的可靠推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as the reasoning backbone of embodied agents, enabling robots to interpret visual scenes, follow language instructions, and plan multi-step actions. In household environments, however, safety depends not only on recognizing objects, but also on how actions change the physical scene over time. Existing embodied safety evaluations largely focus on static risk recognition, unsafe instruction refusal, or final-state task completion. As a result, process-level safety failures induced by spatial relations such as support, containment, and proximity remain insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce SAFERELBENCH, a spatial-relation-aware safety benchmark with 507 executable evaluation samples, including 248 spatial-relation samples and 259 non-spatial control samples. Using SAFERELBENCH to evaluate seven open- and closed-source VLM-driven embodied agents, we find a substantial gap between task success and process-level safety compliance: models often complete the requested task while violating process-level safety constraints. Unlike prior benchmarks, SAFERELBENCH explicitly tests whether agents satisfy safety conditions before risk-prone actions, making spatial relations a core dimension in embodied safety assessment. More broadly, our results show that safe embodied intelligence requires not only stronger perception and planning, but also reliable reasoning about how object relations shape risk during interaction.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Implicit Force: Evaluating Explicit Force-Torque Proxies in Action Chunking with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14578)
*King Hang Wong,Lingqiao Liu,Feras Dayoub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT)在接触丰富的操作任务中之所以表现出力感知能力，很大程度上依赖于遥操作中领导-跟随轨迹误差这一隐式线索。当消除该线索后，ACT在力关键阶段严重失败。而通过简单的关节力矩代理（电机电流或关节力）即可恢复鲁棒的接触行为，甚至超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究ACT在接触丰富操作中表现出的"力感知"能力的真正来源，验证是否依赖于遥操作中领导指令与跟随执行之间的跟踪误差这一隐式接触线索。

Method: 提出一种以观测为中心的ACT变体，预测跟随者关节状态而非领导者命令，以消除遥操作差异信号。并在四个真实世界任务（表面跟随、插入、刚度判别、基于力的停止）中评估关节力矩代理的有效性。

Result: 消除隐式线索后ACT在力关键阶段严重失败；加入力矩代理后鲁棒恢复接触行为，并提升基础ACT策略性能。

Conclusion: 遥操作隐式线索是可恢复的力感知来源；在有力矩信号时，简单的力矩代理可以匹配、超越甚至进一步增强该线索的效果。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to infer interaction state from signals that are often weakly observable through vision and kinematics alone. Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) has shown strong performance in fine-grained manipulation, but many deployments collect demonstrations through leader-follower teleoperation, where tracking error between commanded leader motion and executed follower motion implicitly encodes contact, resistance, and constraint violation. This paper examines whether ACT's apparent force-awareness depends on this hidden interaction cue. We introduce an observation-centric ACT variant that predicts future follower joint states instead of leader commands, thereby removing the teleoperation-induced discrepancy signal while preserving the rest of the learning pipeline. We then evaluate whether simple joint-torque proxies, derived from onboard motor current or joint effort, can recover contact-aware behavior without external force/torque sensors. Across four real-world tasks spanning surface following, insertion, stiffness discrimination, and force-based stopping, removing the implicit cue leads to severe failures in force-critical phases. In contrast, torque-augmented policies recover robust contact behavior and improve the base ACT policy. These results demonstrate that, on real hardware, the implicit teleoperation cue is a recoverable source of force-awareness, where torque signals are available, a simple proxy matches, surpasses, or further enhances it.

</details>


### [96] [SoftNav: Injecting 3D Scene Tokens into VLMs for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14586)
*Yi Wu,Junjie An,Xiao Liu,Yiqun Zhou,Yuechen Wu,Xiaoqing Guan,Shuyang Yu,You Wang,Guang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftNav通过将3D连续表示（每个检测物体或前沿作为一个软token）注入VLM隐空间，以极小的训练成本（~1200样本，~17M参数，3D编码器和VLM冻结）实现了跨场景、跨数据集、甚至零样本迁移到真实机器人的目标驱动具身导航，在HM3D-OVON上超越所有先前方法，并在GOAT-Bench和SG3D上零样本表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法通过文本序列化向VLM传递3D场景信息，存在表征鸿沟（representation gap），导致导航性能受限。作者通过消融实验证实，直接嵌入级传递显著优于文本序列化格式，因此需要一种更有效的3D-to-VLM信息传递方式。

Method: 提出SoftNav：使用轻量级projector将3D编码器输出的每个检测物体或frontier的连续表示作为软token注入VLM隐空间，3D编码器和VLM保持冻结，仅训练projector。

Result: HM3D-OVON上三个split分别达到74.2%/68.3%/66.7% SR，超越所有先前方法的SR和SPL；零样本迁移到GOAT-Bench（67.2% SR）、SG3D（47.2% s-SR）以及真实机器人部署，无需重新训练或架构修改。

Conclusion: 将3D场景token直接注入VLM能够弥合表征鸿沟，实现以极小训练成本即可获得可迁移的导航策略。

Abstract: In goal-directed embodied navigation, where an agent must locate a specified target in an unseen environment, 3D scene understanding and navigation reasoning must work in concert. Current approaches transmit 3D scene information to vision-language models (VLMs) through text, suggesting a representation gap in our tested configurations; a controlled ablation confirms that direct embedding-level transfer significantly outperforms the evaluated text serialization formats. We introduce SoftNav, which injects entity-level 3D continuous representations -- one token per detected object or frontier -- into a VLM's hidden space as soft tokens through a lightweight projector. With the 3D encoder and VLM frozen, only ~1,200 samples and ~17M trainable parameters are needed. On HM3D-OVON, SoftNav achieves 74.2%/68.3%/66.7% SR across three splits, surpassing all prior methods in both SR and SPL; the same navigation policy transfers zero-shot to GOAT-Bench (67.2% SR), SG3D (47.2% s-SR), and real-world robot deployment without retraining or architectural modification. Injecting 3D scene tokens directly into VLMs bridges the representation gap, enabling transferable navigation with minimal training.

</details>


### [97] [Representation-Aligned Tactile Grounding for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14609)
*Ruilin Chen,Jingkai Jia,Tong Yang,Xinyu Zhou,Qiao Sun,Jiangwei Zhong,Shizeng Zhang,Nuo Chen,Bailin He,Wei Li,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在触觉预测器（LTP），通过将未来触觉嵌入对齐到中间动作-专家特征，实现触觉增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，在接触丰富的真实机器人操作任务中取得更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的操作任务中，关键交互状态常被视觉遮挡，未来触觉预测有望提供动作诱导接触动力学的监督信号，但VLA策略中包含感知编码到运动预测等不同角色的表示，不清楚触觉监督应作用于哪个表示层。

Method: 通过线性探针分析发现未来触觉状态最可从中间动作-专家特征预测；据此提出LTP，从该中间表示预测紧凑的未来触觉嵌入，避免直接预测含噪原始触觉信号，提供动作-结果对齐信号。

Result: 在真实世界接触丰富操作任务上，表示对齐的触觉监督优于未对齐或多接口触觉预测方法。

Conclusion: 触觉监督在VLA策略中的应用位置至关重要，对齐中间动作表示与未来接触后果能有效提升接触丰富操作性能。

Abstract: Tactile-enhanced vision-language-action (VLA) policies have been introduced for contact-rich manipulation, where critical interaction states are often hidden from vision. Future tactile prediction is a promising way to use touch because it turns tactile outcomes into supervision for action-induced contact dynamics. Yet VLA policies contain representations with different roles, from perceptual encoding to motor prediction, making it unclear where this supervision should be applied. We study this as a representation-alignment problem. Through a linear probe analysis, we find that future tactile states are most predictable from intermediate action-expert features, rather than from vision-language features or final action states. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a lightweight Latent Tactile Predictor (LTP), which predicts compact future tactile embeddings from the identified intermediate representation. By avoiding direct prediction of noisy raw tactile signals, LTP provides an action-outcome grounding signal that aligns intermediate action representations with future contact consequences. Experiments on real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks show that representation-aligned tactile grounding outperforms less aligned or multi-interface tactile prediction, highlighting the importance of where tactile supervision is applied.

</details>


### [98] [Image-to-Point Cloud Registration Made Easy with Rectified Flow-based LiDAR Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14639)
*Reon Tabata,Kenji Koide,Shuji Oishi,Masashi Yokozuka,Taku Okawara,Aoki Takanose,Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将LiDAR视为成像传感器的方法，通过条件整流流（Conditional Rectified Flow）从稀疏LiDAR扫描生成密集强度图像，再与相机图像进行特征匹配，经PnP-RANSAC估计6自由度位姿。模型通过自监督图像补全预训练+少量LiDAR数据微调，无需图像-点云对或真实传感器位姿。在R3LIVE数据集上平均误差4.89°/1.63m，单次配准约0.68s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到点云配准（I2P）中模态差异大，现有方法难以同时实现高精度与强泛化能力。

Method: 1) 用条件整流流从单帧稀疏LiDAR扫描生成密集LiDAR强度图像；2) 用预训练特征匹配器匹配相机图像与生成的强度图像；3) 通过PnP-RANSAC估计6自由度位姿。训练采用自监督图像补全预训练+少量LiDAR数据微调，无需跨模态配对。

Result: 在R3LIVE数据集上平均误差4.89°/1.63m，优于现有方法；单次配准耗时约0.68s。

Conclusion: 将LiDAR视为成像传感器，通过生成强度图像桥接模态差异，实现高效、泛化的跨模态配准，且训练数据需求低。

Abstract: Image-to-Point Cloud Registration (I2P) is essential for integrating camera and LiDAR in perception and autonomous systems, yet the modality gap between images and point clouds makes it difficult to achieve both high accuracy and strong generalization. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective I2P method that treats LiDAR as an imaging sensor: from a single sparse LiDAR scan, we generate a dense LiDAR intensity image using Conditional Rectified Flow, match it with a camera image using a pre-trained feature matcher, and estimate the 6-DoF relative pose via PnP-RANSAC. The proposed model is pre-trained through a self-supervised image completion task and fine-tuned on a small amount of LiDAR data (neither image-point cloud pairs nor ground-truth sensor poses are required), enabling it to scale to diverse LiDAR and camera configurations. Experiments on the R3LIVE dataset show that the proposed method achieves a mean error of 4.89° / 1.63 m, outperforming existing methods, while completing a single registration in approximately 0.68 s.

</details>


### [99] [NavCMPO: Critic-Guided MeanFlow Policy Optimization for Adaptive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14643)
*Junjie An,Yi Wu,Xiao Liu,Yiqun Zhou,Yuechen Wu,Xiaoqing Guan,You Wang,Guang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavCMPO是一个两阶段自适应导航框架，结合少步MeanFlow轨迹生成、Critic引导精化和强化学习微调，在InternVLA-N1基准上成功率74.7%（比NavDP高6.4%），推理延迟从85ms降至60ms，并在Unitree Go2上验证了sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端扩散策略导航方法存在推理延迟高的问题（迭代去噪过程慢），且行为克隆受限于专家演示质量，障碍物感知能力不足。

Method: 1) 预训练阶段：障碍物接近度预测任务增强视觉表征；2) 少步MeanFlow轨迹生成 + Critic-Guided Trajectory Refinement (CGTR) 使用障碍物点云监督的critic梯度精化中间轨迹；3) 适应阶段：PPO+行为克隆正则化微调MeanFlow策略，同时更新critic以适应具体embodiment变化。

Result: InternVLA-N1基准上成功率74.7%，超过NavDP基线6.4个百分点；推理延迟从85ms降至60ms；Unitree Go2上成功实现sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: NavCMPO通过结合扩散模型少步生成、critic引导精化和RL微调，有效平衡了导航性能与推理效率，并验证了sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: End-to-end diffusion-based policies have demonstrated strong performance in mapless visual navigation, but their iterative denoising process introduces substantial inference latency, while behavior cloning limits performance to the quality of expert demonstrations. We present NavCMPO, a two-stage adaptive navigation framework that combines few-step MeanFlow trajectory generation, critic-guided refinement, and reinforcement learning fine-tuning. During pre-training, an obstacle proximity prediction task encourages the visual representation to capture obstacle-aware spatial information. To compensate for the degradation in obstacle avoidance caused by few-step generation, Critic-Guided Trajectory Refinement (CGTR) uses gradients from a critic trained with obstacle-point-cloud supervision to refine intermediate trajectories. During adaptation, the MeanFlow policy is fine-tuned using Proximal Policy Optimization with behavior-cloning regularization, while the critic is updated to accommodate embodiment-specific observation changes. Under a matched training budget on the InternVLA-N1 benchmark, NavCMPO achieves an average success rate of 74.7\%, exceeding the retrained NavDP baseline by 6.4 percentage points, while reducing inference latency from 85\,ms to 60\,ms. Experiments on a Unitree Go2 further demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [100] [An Intelligent-Cloud Edge Multimodal Interaction System for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14675)
*Zihan Guo,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种云边协同多模态交互框架，集成改进YOLO-DC手势检测器（CBAM+DIoU）与LLM/VLM智能体，在资源受限机器人上实现高精度手势感知与复杂任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境下人机交互需要准确的手势感知、语义场景理解及可靠的任务规划，但机器人端算力有限，亟需轻量高效且能协同云端大模型的解决方案。

Method: 提出YOLO-DC检测器（在Neck中加入CBAM注意力模块、用DIoU Loss替代原边界框回归损失）；构建云端-边缘双层级：云端负责手势检测、场景理解、多模态融合与动作规划，TonyPi机器人本地负责数据采集、通信、动作执行与反馈。

Result: YOLO-DC在公开手势数据集上精度98.9%、mAP@0.5=90.7%；自建数据集精度95.0%、mAP@0.5=92.7%。系统任务成功率：单动作95%、复合动作88%、视觉依赖任务82%。30人用户满意度均分3.69/5。

Conclusion: 结合改进手势检测与多模态LLM/VLM智能体的云边架构在资源受限机器人交互场景中可行且有效。

Abstract: Robust human-robot interaction in complex environments requires accurate gesture perception, semantic scene understanding, and reliable task planning under limited onboard computing resources. This paper presents a cloud-edge multimodal interaction framework that integrates an enhanced YOLO-based gesture detector with coordinated large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) agents. The proposed detector, incorporates the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) into the neck and replaces the baseline bounding-box regression objective with Distance-IoU (DIoU) loss. These modifications improve feature discrimination and localization for small or partially occluded gestures in complex backgrounds. The cloud layer performs gesture detection, scene understanding, multimodal fusion, and action planning, whereas the TonyPi robot locally handles data acquisition, communication, action execution, and feedback. Experiments on a public gesture dataset and a custom dataset show that YOLO-DC achieves precision values of 98.9% and 95.0%, with mAP@0.5 values of 90.7% and 92.7%, respectively. System-level evaluation yields success rates of 95%, 88%, and 82% for single-action, composite-action, and vision-dependent tasks. A 30 participant evaluation yields an overall mean satisfaction score of 3.69 out of 5. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining refined gesture detection with multimodal agents for resource-constrained robotic interaction.

</details>


### [101] [MIND-CAVs: Multi-Intelligence Negotiation and Decision System for CAVs based on Intent-Driven Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14688)
*Mainak Mondal,Yihang Feng,Yangchao Luo,Han Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MIND-CAVs框架，通过V2X通信将车辆原始感知抽象为结构化意图表示，边缘服务器进行意图协商仲裁，实现网联自动驾驶车辆的全局一致协调决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶车辆作为孤立智能体运行，仅广播低级运动学状态，缺乏机制进行全局一致的、考虑意图的协调决策。

Method: 每辆车将感知抽象为结构化意图表示，通过V2X交换，边缘服务器结合学习与规则仲裁机制协商冲突意图，云端记录决策用于审计和持续训练。

Result: 在CARLA多车道高速场景中，相比孤立自动驾驶、先到先服务仲裁和多智能体强化学习基线，改善了机动完成时间，减少了不安全接近和不必要制动。

Conclusion: 意图驱动的协商决策框架能有效提升网联自动驾驶的协调效率与安全性。

Abstract: Modern autonomous vehicles largely operate as isolated agents: they rely on on-board perception and decision modules and broadcast Basic Safety Messages (BSMs) that expose only low-level kinematic state. While existing cooperative driving frameworks enable limited sensor sharing, they rarely communicate high-level maneuver intentions, and edge computing is primarily used for content delivery rather than decision arbitration. As a result, current connected autonomy lacks a principled mechanism for making globally consistent, intent-aware coordination decisions across vehicles. To address this gap, we propose MIND-CAVs, a Multi-Intelligence Negotiation and Decision framework for connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs) based on intent-driven autonomy. Each vehicle abstracts raw sensor observations into structured intent representations, exchanges them over V2X links, and receives globally consistent coordination plans from roadside edge servers. Edge agents combine learned and rule-based arbitration mechanisms to negotiate conflicting intents among vehicles, while a cloud platform records decisions for auditing and continual retraining. We implement MIND-CAVs in a CARLA-based AI-in-the-loop platform and evaluate it in multi-lane highway scenarios involving conflicting maneuvers and route-constrained exits. Experimental results show improved maneuver completion time and reduced unsafe proximity and unnecessary braking compared with isolated autonomy, first-come-first-served arbitration, and multi-agent reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Reflex: Real-Time VLA Control through Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14695)
*Yuanchun Guo,Bingyan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Reflex通过利用流匹配策略中感知编码器与去噪循环的功能独立性（时间步不变性），将注意力上下文划分为静态、滑动和动态区域，实现O(1)增量缓存更新，结合AdaRMSNorm抗数值坍塌、异步流水线解耦视觉编码与动作生成、算子融合等优化，在LIBERO和Kinetix上达到2.58倍加速和50Hz稳定流式推理，延迟降低54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA模型的迭代去噪本质与实时机器人部署存在根本冲突：全局时间步注入使KV缓存失效，面临O(N²)重计算或数学上错误的缓存复用二选一的困境，亟需一种能兼容流匹配且支持实时流式推理的框架。

Method: 1) 利用时间步不变性将注意力上下文分区为静态/滑动/动态区域，实现O(1)增量缓存更新。2) AdaRMSNorm自适应归一化层，根据流相位门控防止BFloat16数值坍塌。3) 异步流水线解耦视觉编码与动作生成。4) 算子融合减少内核开销。

Result: LIBERO和Kinetix基准上：2.58倍推理加速，50Hz稳定流式推理，反应延迟降低54%，无性能退化。

Conclusion: Reflex通过系统级优化解决了流匹配VLA的实时推理瓶颈，证明了在保持全batch等价输出的前提下实现高效流式部署的可行性。

Abstract: Flow matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise precise continuous control, but their iterative denoising nature introduces fundamental incompatibilities with real-time robotics: global timestep injection invalidates KV-caching, forcing a choice between slow $O(N^2)$ re-computation or mathematically incorrect cache reuse. We present \textbf{Reflex}, a framework that enables \textit{real-time streaming inference} for flow matching policies by exploiting the \textit{Timestep-Invariance Property} -- that perception encoders are functionally independent of the denoising loop. Reflex partitions the attention context into static, sliding, and dynamic regions, enabling $O(1)$ incremental cache updates while preserving full-batch-equivalent attention outputs for fixed inputs. To ensure stability under continuous high-frequency inference, we introduce \textit{AdaRMSNorm}, an adaptive normalization layer that prevents BFloat16 numerical collapse by gating on flow phase. We further maximize throughput through an \textit{async pipeline} that decouples visual encoding from action generation, combined with \textit{operator fusion} that reduces kernel overhead. On LIBERO and Kinetix benchmarks, Reflex achieves a 2.58$\times$ inference speedup and 50Hz stable streaming, reducing reaction latency by up to 54\% and enabling efficient deployment without performance degradation.

</details>


### [103] [Lights, Camera, Malfunction: When Illumination Robustness Leaves VLA Models Blind to Color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14698)
*Marino Watanabe,Takami Sato,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FLARE物理光照攻击框架，可在黑盒条件下使VLA任务成功率降至0%；发现对抗训练中的防御陷阱——数据增强会导致VLA模型丢失颜色感知退化为形状偏置；提出ChromaGuard保色对抗训练方法，在真实6-DoF机械臂上保持高等成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实环境中对微小环境扰动存在脆弱性，现有对抗训练方法存在未被充分认识的防御缺陷——错误地将颜色信息作为噪声丢弃，导致模型感知退化。

Method: 提出FLARE物理聚光灯攻击框架（黑盒、无需模型内部访问）；通过诊断性灰度评估揭示对抗训练导致的颜色感知退化；提出ChromaGuard保色对抗训练方法。

Result: FLARE攻击使基线任务成功率为零；标准对抗训练后，灰度输入保持高成功率但真实彩色任务成功率降至≤47.5%；ChromaGuard在物理机器人平台上达到97.5%（良性）和92.5%（攻击下）的成功率。

Conclusion: VLA模型的颜色感知是关键但易被忽视的脆弱维度，ChromaGuard有效解决了对抗训练中的颜色退化问题，防御物理光照攻击。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for general-purpose robot manipulation; however, their transition to real-world environments reveals vulnerabilities to minor environmental perturbations. We propose FLARE, an optimized physical spotlight attack framework that exploits these vulnerabilities via targeted illuminations, dropping baseline task success rates to zero without any access to model internals. While adversarial training is the standard countermeasure, we identify a critical and previously underestimated defensive pitfall: naive data augmentations incorrectly condition VLA models to discard color as noise, collapsing their visual perception into a purely shape-biased processor. We expose this degradation through a diagnostic grayscale evaluation, in which the defended model maintains high success rates on grayscale inputs, while its success rate on benign, color-dependent real-world tasks drops to at most 47.5%, well below the undefended baseline. To address this, we propose ChromaGuard, a chroma-preserving adversarial training method. On a physical 6-DoF robotic platform, we demonstrate that ChromaGuard achieves 97.5% and 92.5% success rates in benign and attacked color-dependent tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [104] [Reinforcement Learning for the Full Strawberry Harvesting Process: Obstacle Separation, Detachment, and Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14708)
*Changyou Miao,Teng Li,Ya Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种策略驱动的强化学习框架，结合启发式相位协调，实现了机器人草莓采摘中障碍分离、果实摘取和放置的顺序决策，通过分层架构、共享观测空间和零样本sim-to-real迁移，在仿真和真实实验中分别达到89.7%和82.0%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严重遮挡和可变形植物结构带来的复杂接触动力学是机器人草莓采摘的主要挑战，现有方法难以处理这些动态交互。

Method: 策略驱动RL框架+启发式相位协调，将采摘分解为障碍分离、果实摘取、放置三个顺序决策阶段；共享交互感知策略生成笛卡尔运动；轻量启发式逻辑协调阶段推进和夹爪事件；共享结构化观测空间；分层架构（高层策略+低层笛卡尔阻抗控制）；可行性优先的观测对齐和域随机化实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 仿真成功率89.7%，真实实验成功率82.0%；遮挡等级从1增至5时，平均执行时间从12.99秒升至21.73秒。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将交互感知的采摘行为迁移到结构不同的机器人平台，验证了零样本sim-to-real的有效性。

Abstract: Severe occlusions and deformable plant structures introduce complex contact dynamics that challenge robotic strawberry harvesting. A policy-driven reinforcement learning (RL) framework with heuristic phase coordination was developed, in which obstacle separation, fruit detachment, and placement were formulated as a sequential decision-making task. A shared interaction-aware policy generated Cartesian motions across all task phases, while lightweight heuristic logic coordinated task progression and gripper events. A shared structured observation space was used to represent target, obstacle, end-effector, and task-context information. A hierarchical architecture combined the high-level policy with low-level Cartesian impedance control for compliant interaction. To support zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, feasibility-first observation alignment and domain randomization were adopted. The policy achieved success rates of 89.7% in simulation and 82.0% in real-world experiments. As the occlusion level increased from 1 to 5, the average execution time increased from 12.99 s to 21.73 s, reflecting greater interaction complexity. These results demonstrated effective transfer of interaction-aware harvesting behaviors to a structurally different robotic platform.

</details>


### [105] [BridgeFlow: Fast and Robust SE(2)-Equivariant Motion Planning with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14725)
*Xinzhe Zhou,Xuyang Wang,Xiaoming Duan,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeFlow提出了一种快速、严格SE(2)-等变生成式运动规划框架，通过轻量级任务中心规范化模块实现精确空间等变性，结合布朗桥先验和上下文感知小批量最优传输加速推理，在密集2D环境和7-DoF Franka机械臂上实现最高15倍推理加速和2倍有效轨迹率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的运动规划器要么缺乏固有的等变性（将变换后的任务视为新场景），要么通过计算昂贵的专门架构强制等变性而阻碍实时推理。需要打破这种权衡。

Method: 采用轻量级任务中心规范化（canonicalization）模块实现精确SE(2)等变性，使用标准架构即可；引入布朗桥信息先验（Brownian bridge informative prior）和上下文感知小批量最优传输（context-aware mini-batch optimal transport）构建平直化向量场；通过Classifier-Free Guidance嵌入环境感知。

Result: 在密集2D环境和7-DoF Franka机械臂上，相比最先进的扩散基线，推理速度提升最高15倍，有效轨迹率提升2倍，且能鲁棒泛化到完全未见的环境和任意空间变换。

Conclusion: BridgeFlow通过轻量规范化而非重等变网络实现了严格等变性与高效推理的统一，为实时机器人运动规划提供了实用且可泛化的生成式方案。

Abstract: In robotic motion planning, equivariance to rigid body transformations is crucial for robust spatial generalization. However, current learning-based planners face a critical dilemma: they either lack inherent equivariance, treating transformed tasks as novel scenarios, or enforce it via computationally expensive specialized architectures that bottleneck real-time inference. To break this trade-off, we propose BridgeFlow, a fast and strictly SE(2)-equivariant generative motion planning framework. Rather than relying on heavy equivariant networks, BridgeFlow achieves exact spatial equivariance via a lightweight task-centric canonicalization module, enabling generalization using standard architectures. To further accelerate inference, we pair a Brownian bridge informative prior with context-aware mini-batch optimal transport. This constructs a straightened vector field that minimizes transport costs and stabilizes training. Furthermore, environmental awareness is explicitly embedded via Classifier-Free Guidance. Evaluations in dense 2D environments and on a 7-DoF Franka manipulator demonstrate that BridgeFlow achieves up to a 15x inference speedup and a 2x higher valid trajectory rate over state-of-the-art diffusion baselines, alongside robust generalization to entirely unseen environments and arbitrary spatial transformations.

</details>


### [106] [Hybrid Rigid-Soft Robotic Gripper with Shape Adaptation, Uniform Force Distribution, and Self-Locking Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14730)
*Xi Chen,Yun Wang,Lichao Yang,Haitao Li,Ya Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合刚-软机器人夹爪，结合低成本膜式气动执行器和3D打印双棘轮机构，实现形状自适应、均匀力分布和能量自锁，负载4200g、能耗降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人夹爪在农业自动化中难以兼顾柔性自适应抓取、关节压力平衡和高负载能力，且常伴随高能耗。

Method: 将膜式气动执行器与3D打印双棘轮-棘爪机构集成，双棘轮偏移配置提升关节锁定角度分辨率，利用自锁机制消除连续气压需求。

Result: 最大负载4200g（远超软体夹爪45-210g）；力分布差异率1.75-35.29%（刚性夹爪56.77-66.44%）；单次抓取能耗42.6J（对比85.28J，降低50.05%）。

Conclusion: 该夹爪成功弥合了软体柔顺性与刚性可靠性之间的鸿沟，为可扩展的农业采摘和操作任务提供了鲁棒高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers face a significant challenge in agricultural automation: the trade-off between compliant, adaptive grasping, pressure balancing among all joints, and high load capacity, often at the cost of high energy consumption. This paper presents a novel hybrid rigid-soft gripper that integrated low-cost, membrane-based pneumatic actuators with 3D-printed dual ratchet-pawl mechanisms to simultaneously achieve shape adaptation, uniform force distribution, and energy-free self-locking. The dual-ratchet structure assembled in an offset configuration significantly increased the angular resolution of the joint locking mechanism. Key experimental results demonstrated the gripper's superior performance: a remarkable maximum load capacity of 4200 g, far exceeding that of conventional soft grippers (45-210 g); more uniform force distribution across object sizes (1.75-35.29% difference ratio) compared to a rigid gripper (56.77-66.44%), with peak contact forces remaining below surface damage thresholds; and a 50.05% reduction in total energy consumption to 42.6 J per grasp cycle, achieved by eliminating the need for continuous pneumatic pressure through the self-locking mechanism, compared to 85.28 J for a conventional soft gripper. The combination of additive manufacturing for ratchets and commercially available materials for pneumatic chambers ensured a low-cost and easily fabricated design. These findings validated that the proposed gripper successfully bridged the gap between soft compliance and rigid reliability, offering a robust and efficient solution for scalable agricultural harvesting and manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Curvature-Constrained and Constant-Speed Distributed Simultaneous Arrival Control for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14781)
*Zhouru Xiao,Yang Lu,Weijia Yao,Min Liu,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对固定翼无人机等曲率受限、速度恒定的多机器人系统，提出一种基于最大一致性协议的分布式切换控制方法，利用Dubins路径几何特性与优化原理，引入虚拟时间变量，结合最优控制与饱和比例控制的混合控制律，使每个机器人趋近邻居的最大虚拟时间，从而在温和条件下实现同时到达，并在某些情况下达到理论最优到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人同时到达目标点是协同包围、灾难救援等任务的关键，但现有方法难以同时处理轨迹曲率约束、各机器人恒定速度（可能不同）以及分布式控制这三个实际约束，而这些约束对固定翼无人机等系统是典型且必要的。

Method: 提出分布式切换控制方法：基于最大一致性协议，利用Dubins路径几何特性与优化原理，引入虚拟时间变量，设计混合控制律（最优控制+饱和比例控制），使每个机器人向邻居最大虚拟时间趋近。

Result: 理论证明在某些情况下可达到理论最优到达时间；方法可扩展、实时、通信开销低；通过大量仿真和实验验证了有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能同时处理轨迹曲率约束、异质恒定速度和分布式控制的多机器人同时到达方法，具有理论最优性保证和良好的实用性。

Abstract: The simultaneous arrival of multiple mobile robots at a target point is crucial for cooperation tasks such as cooperative encirclement, disaster relief, and environmental monitoring. Although the simultaneous arrival problem itself is already complex, the problem becomes more challenging when there are constraints on the robot trajectory curvatures and the speeds are required to be constant (possibly different for different robots), and the control law for robots needs to be distributed. These constraints are typical for a multi-robot system consisting of, e.g., fixed-wing UAVs. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a distributed switching control method based on the maximum consensus protocol. By exploiting the geometric properties of Dubins paths along with optimization principles, a virtual time variable is introduced, and a hybrid control law that combines optimal control with saturated proportional control is designed. Under the proposed control law, each robot is driven to approach the maximum virtual time among its neighbors, thereby achieving simultaneous arrival under some mild conditions. Furthermore, we prove that in certain cases the proposed method attains a theoretically optimal arrival time. The approach is scalable and real-time, with low communication overhead. Its effectiveness and robustness are validated through extensive simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [108] [Interventional Causal Circuits for Safe Robot Action Testing and Failure Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14826)
*Naren Vasantakumaar,Tom Schierenbeck,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合联合概率树(JPT)与因果回路的闭环框架，在运动规划被拒绝时进行因果诊断，找出失败原因参数并推荐修正值，实现无需重训或额外数据的可解释安全测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI机器人动作需要在执行前通过安全测试，但正式测试计算成本高且随动作空间维度扩展性差。当动作被拒绝时，盲目重采样效率低、不收敛、无信息量。

Method: 构建联合概率树(JPT)与基于边际确定性变量树的因果回路，实现闭式因果推理：被拒绝时自动诊断失败原因变量，计算使测试通过概率最大化的干预修正值。所有干预查询在运行前验证可计算性，自动排除越界候选。

Result: 在ROS2仿真中，高质量JPT下减少10.3%的失败尝试，低质量JPT下减少37%。每个被拒计划生成结构化因果报告（主因变量、观测值、推荐修正区间）。

Conclusion: 闭环因果诊断优于盲目重采样，提供可解释的故障归因与修正建议，支持人工监督与自主恢复，无需额外训练故障模型。

Abstract: Safe physical AI for robot actions are required not only likely to succeed but tested to be safe before execution. In practice, however, formal testing of motion parameters is computationally expensive, and the cost scales poorly with the dimensionality of the action space. When a proposed action is rejected by a tester, the naive response is to resample blindly until a passing candidate is found. This is wasteful, uninformative, and offers no convergence. We argue that rejection should instead trigger causal diagnosis: a principled identification of which action parameter caused the failure and what corrective value maximises the probability of passing testing under the interventional probability distribution. We propose a closed-loop framework that couples a Joint Probability Tree (JPT) with a Causal Circuit derived from a Marginal-Deterministic Variable Tree, enabling exact polytime computation without retraining, or additional data collection. The framework validates tractability of all interventional queries before the robot begins operating, and out-of-support candidates are detected and excluded from correction automatically. We perform experiments in a ROS2 simulation environment, and the framework demonstrates complementary roles across quality of distribution: under a high-quality JPT, the Causal Circuit reduces failed attempts by 10.3% and under a degraded JPT, it reduces total failed attempts by 37%. Every rejected plan produces a structured, interpretable causal report naming the primary cause variable, its observed value, and the recommended corrective region, supporting operator oversight and autonomous recovery without a separately trained failure model.

</details>


### [109] [KineFuse: Kinematic-Aware Haptic Fusion for In-Hand Occluded-Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14842)
*Chanyoung Ahn,Jaesung Lee,Sungwoo Park,Donghyun Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种运动学感知的手指级编码器，利用手指已有的稀疏触觉信号（本体感受、力/力矩、二值接触）来增强预训练视觉位姿跟踪器在遮挡条件下的性能，通过三种层次的评估验证了顺序跟踪优于逐帧评估、编码器学会无监督的跨模态门控、4 token的手指级表征优于扁平融合和关节级表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手内操作需要连续6D位姿跟踪，但操作手指会不可避免地遮挡物体，现有视觉跟踪器在严重遮挡下性能下降。研究如何利用多指手上已可获取的稀疏触觉信号来补充视觉跟踪，解决遮挡问题。

Method: 提出一种运动学感知的手指级编码器（kinematic-aware finger-level encoder），将本体感受、力/力矩、二值触觉信号编码为4个finger token，与视觉特征融合实现跨模态门控。系统性对比了四种备选设计，并进行了逐帧精化、顺序开环跟踪、闭环操作三个层次的评估。

Result: (i) 逐帧评估无法区分编码器质量，顺序跟踪将架构差异放大至15倍；(ii) 结构化编码器无监督学会任务特定的跨模态门控：视觉专用于平移，一个注意力头专用于触觉处理旋转；(iii) 4 token的手指级表征优于扁平融合和关节级表征。改进后的跟踪在重定向任务中取得更高成功率。

Conclusion: 灵巧手内操作的触觉-视觉融合跟踪中，编码器结构设计至关重要；顺序评估比逐帧评估更能反映真实性能；紧凑的 finger-level tokenization 是更优的设计选择。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires continuous 6D pose tracking, yet the manipulating fingers inevitably occlude the object from the camera. We study how to structure the sparse haptic signals already available on multi-fingered hands, including proprioception, proximal force/torque, and binary contact, to complement a pretrained visual pose tracker under occlusion.
